In this paper, we review the original asynchronous dynamics for Hopfield networks and present a new tool and a new synchronous dynamics with its theoretical justification and four computational experiments to assess the speed up in processing time empirically.

</details>


### [236] [Baikal: Structured Search for Deep Research over Data Lakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27726)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Rishitha Guttapalle Mohan,Aarti Kumari,Ashi Sinha,Athulya Anil,Kavitha Srinivas,Horst Samulowitz,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Baikal框架，通过将数据湖中的异构证据聚类为语义区域并自适应搜索，在固定预算下平衡探索与利用，显著提升深度研究报告质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代检索生成方法易过度利用局部证据，难以覆盖不同语义区域，导致预算受限时报告质量不佳。

Method: 将深度研究建模为预算受限搜索问题，先聚类证据为语义区域，再通过区域级价值估计和多种选择策略（随机、LLM引导、贝叶斯ε-greedy、UCB）自适应搜索，并在区域内生成子问题以获取奖励更新估值。

Result: 在HybridQA和TAT-QA数据湖上（含超万张表和大量段落），最佳配置较最强基线分别提升28%和36%的报告分数，且提升源于更好的groundedness和diversity。

Conclusion: 结构化语义区域探索能系统性地提升异构数据湖上的研究质量，验证了探索与利用平衡策略的有效性。

Abstract: Deep research over data lakes requires an LLM agent to investigate evidence across thousands of heterogeneous tables and passages to synthesize a report. Existing methods perform iterative retrieval and generation, letting accumulated context determine what to investigate next, which can overexploit locally promising evidence and fail to cover distinct semantic regions under a fixed budget. To address this, we cast deep research over data lakes as a budgeted search problem and present Baikal - a framework that clusters heterogeneous evidence into semantic regions, then searches over them adaptively to balance exploration and exploitation. Within each selected region, Baikal generates and investigates region-grounded subquestions, using finding quality as rewards to update region-level value estimates and guide search under policies ranging from random and LLM-guided selection to Bayesian $ε$-greedy and UCB. We evaluate Baikal on 15 queries each over HybridQA and TAT-QA data lakes containing 10,993 and 2,757 tables, respectively, together with 227K Wikipedia passages and 13K financial report passages. We assess research quality with a new rubric covering groundedness, relevance, diversity, and utility, and use GPT-5-mini to score Baikal and strong baselines, including DeepSearcher and an OpenCode research agent with retrieval and clustering variants. Across both data lakes, Baikal performs strongly under several region-selection policies; its best configuration improves report scores over the strongest baselines by 28% on HybridQA and 36% on TAT-QA. Our analyses attribute these gains to organizing and exploring semantic evidence regions, which improves groundedness and diversity and yields more useful findings under the same subquestion budget. These results demonstrate the value of structured semantic exploration for systematic research and discovery over heterogeneous data lakes.

</details>


### [237] [STEREODISCO: Discovering Stereotypicality in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27824)
*Farane Jalali Farahani,Corina Dima,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Raphael H. Heiberger,Steffen Staab*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出STEREODISCO框架，系统性地从LLM内部表征中挖掘语义轴上的刻板印象，发现模型间一致性高于与人类的一致性，并识别出前人未研究的新刻板印象轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注少量社会心理学语义轴，且局限于词嵌入层面，无法全面揭示LLM中哪些语义轴承载刻板印象及其内部表征方式。

Method: 基于Osgood的语义区分法，从WordNet反义词集构建约2000个候选语义轴；通过探针在激活空间中恢复几何轴，并利用统计检验识别概念投影中的刻板印象轴。

Result: LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT和MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT在社交群体评分上相互一致性高于与人类的一致性，并发现如谦逊-骄傲、狭隘-豁达、懦弱-勇敢等新刻板轴，经人工验证。

Conclusion: STEREODISCO可有效扩展LLM偏见研究范围，揭示模型独有的刻板内容，为理解与校准LLM社会偏见提供新工具。

Abstract: LLMs encode, convey, and perpetuate stereotypes. Prior computational research focuses on a small set of semantic axes investigated in social psychology, and operates on word embeddings produced by language models, leaving open which other semantic axes carry stereotypical associations in LLMs and how LLMs internally represent such axes. We introduce STEREODISCO, a framework that adapts the semantic differential method (Osgood et al., 1957) to the systematic study of stereotypes in LLM internal representations. STEREODISCO constructs approx. 2,000 candidate semantic axes from WordNet antonym synsets, recovers each as a geometric axis in the LLM's activation space via probing, and identifies stereotypical axes via a statistical test over concept projections. As a case study, we apply STEREODISCO to social group stereotypes with LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT and MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT. We find that the two LLMs agree with each other on social group ratings more than with humans, suggesting that LLM-encoded stereotype content diverges from that documented in social psychology. We also discover stereotypical axes not investigated in prior work -- including humble vs. proud, narrow-minded vs. broad-minded, and cowardly vs. brave, which human annotators independently confirm.

</details>


### [238] [Virtual Process Dossier: A Process-Aware Data Catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27840)
*Lukas Kubelka,Alexander Bott,Frank Döhner,Saksham Kiroriwal,Georg Zeeb,Julia Butte,Julius Pfrommer,Jürgen Beyerer,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出虚拟流程档案（VPD），一种基于知识图谱的数据目录，用于多阶段制造场景，以FAIR方式提供数据集，并显式记录前瞻和追溯工作流来源，包含本体、集成框架和用户界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段制造中的AI优化任务需要区分各工作流步骤生成的数据集，现有方案缺乏对工作流来源的显式建模和FAIR数据管理，导致数据溯源和复用困难。

Method: 构建VPD本体作为语义核心，设计来源框架将本体实例化融入生产环境，开发用户界面实现人机交互，支持前瞻和追溯来源的显式记录。

Result: 提供FAIR化数据集，明确工作流步骤间的来源关系，本体与代码已开源（GitHub），支持实际制造场景的AI优化需求。

Conclusion: VPD通过知识图谱实现多阶段制造数据的规范目录与来源追踪，为下游AI任务提供可靠数据基础，代码开源便于复用与扩展。

Abstract: We propose the Virtual Process Dossier (VPD), a Knowledge Graph-based data catalogue that also captures workflow provenance. We developed VPD for multi-stage manufacturing use-cases where downstream AI-based optimization tasks require to distinct between datasets generated during individual workflow steps. VPD provides these datasets in a FAIR manner and makes both prospective and retrospective workflow provenance explicit. Our contributions are: (1) the VPD ontology that serves as the catalogue's semantic core; (2) the VPD provenance framework that integrates ontology instantiation into the production environment; and (3) the VPD user interface that provides human-centered interaction with the VPD Knowledge Graph. The ontology and code are available at https://github.com/kubeluk/VirtualProcessDossier .

</details>


### [239] [Understanding Submodular Information Measure Based Objectives for Representation Learning: A Variance and Separation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27660)
*Rishabh Iyer,Truong Pham,Anay Majee*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文首次为子模信息度量（SIMs）在表示学习中的几何与统计特性提供了统一理论框架，证明不同SIM目标分别刻画类内结构（如方差、广义方差、不平衡感知）与类间结构（如质心分离、马氏距离、模态重叠），并通过合成实验验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SCORE框架表明子模信息度量可有效用于监督对比学习，但不同SIM诱导的几何与统计性质尚不清楚，缺乏理论指导以选择和设计合适的SIM目标。

Method: 构建统一理论框架，将Total Information（TI）与Mutual Information（MI）两类SIM分别映射到类内与类间结构：针对Graph Cut、LogDet、Facility Location三种具体形式，推导其与经典统计概念（如类内方差、协方差体积、Fisher判别、马氏距离等）的等价或近似关系，并通过独立控制方差、协方差、类别不平衡、类间分离度及多模态重叠的合成实验进行验证。

Result: 理论预测与合成实验行为高度吻合：Graph Cut TI对应类内方差，LogDet TI对应广义方差/协方差体积，Facility Location TI突出稀有易混类的分离；Graph Cut MI关联质心分离与Fisher判别，LogDet MI体现马氏距离的协方差感知分离，Facility Location MI度量最近模态重叠。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个对子模信息度量几何与统计性质的统一理解，为表示学习中SIM目标的选择与设计提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Submodular Information Measures (SIMs) have recently emerged as a powerful framework for representation learning and multimodal learning. In particular, the SCORE framework~\cite{majee2024score} demonstrated that SIMs can serve as effective objectives for supervised contrastive learning. Despite their empirical success, however, the geometric and statistical properties induced by different submodular information measures remain poorly understood.
  In this work, we develop a unified theoretical framework connecting SIMs to classical concepts in representation learning and statistical pattern recognition. We show that Total Information (TI) objectives characterize intra-class structure: Graph Cut TI recovers within-class variance, LogDet TI recovers generalized variance and covariance volume, and Facility Location TI induces imbalance-aware separation that emphasizes rare and confusable classes. We further show that Mutual Information (MI) objectives capture complementary notions of inter-class structure: Graph Cut MI is closely related to centroid separation and Fisher-style discrimination, LogDet MI captures covariance-aware separation through Mahalanobis distance, and Facility Location MI measures nearest-mode representational overlap.
  We validate these theoretical characterizations using controlled synthetic experiments that independently vary variance, covariance, class imbalance, class separation, and multimodal overlap. Across all settings, the empirical behavior closely matches the proposed theory. Our results provide the first unified geometric and statistical understanding of submodular information measures and offer principled guidance for selecting and designing SIM-based objectives for representation learning.

</details>


### [240] [Search as Computation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27871)
*Alexander Tuisov*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文系统性地提出了终端计算分配问题的框架，通过贝尔曼方程刻画最优计算分配，并深入分析了计算价值（VOC）与信息度量的关系，最终展示了该理论如何统一解释多种算法（如A*搜索）的设计原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法常将内部计算消耗视为成本，但评估仅关注最终决策质量，缺乏统一的形式化框架来分析这类问题。作者希望建立一套理论，能够刻画在固定预算、计算定价或精确认证等不同约束下，如何最优地分配计算资源以最小化终端决策损失，并澄清价值计算与信息论之间的关系。

Method: 将问题建模为终端计算分配问题，利用贝尔曼方程推导最优策略；通过对比互信息、简单后悔、知识梯度等不同度量，分析VOC的性质；并针对不同计算拓扑（如多臂老虎机、树搜索、节点扩展）进行建模；最后在显式的边界分辨和启发式误差模型下，推导出加权A*作为最大化近似VOC的结果。

Result: 互信息在对数损失下等价于我的opic VOC，而在简单后悔下VOC是知识梯度量；信息增益可能对VOC排序极差，但提供单侧上界。在特定误差模型下，最大化近似VOC可恢复加权A*，A*和贪心最佳优先搜索是极限情况。

Conclusion: 该理论为多种计算分配策略提供了一个统一的决策视角，但强调不存在普遍最优的获取规则，不同场景下需要根据具体条件选择合适策略。

Abstract: Many algorithms spend an internal resource before returning a decision and are evaluated only by the quality of that terminal output. We formalize such procedures as terminal computation-allocation problems: costly computations produce observations, update beliefs about a latent environment, and matter only through terminal decision loss. Bellman equations characterize optimal allocation under fixed budgets, priced computation, and exact certification. We then relate value of computation (VOC) to information. Mutual information equals myopic VOC under log loss, whereas under simple regret VOC is a knowledge-gradient quantity; moreover, information gain can rank computations arbitrarily poorly, although it gives a one-sided upper bound on VOC. Bandit pulls, tree simulations, and node expansions illustrate the same model under different computation topologies. Finally, under an explicit frontier-resolution and heuristic-error model, maximizing approximate VOC recovers weighted A*, with A* and greedy best-first search as limiting cases. The theory identifies a shared decision problem without asserting that one acquisition rule is universally optimal.

</details>


### [241] [Event-Structured Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Differentiable Critical Clearing Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27681)
*Baoli Hao,Chenxi Hu,Ming Zhong,Ren Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出事件结构化物理信息神经网络（ES-PINN），用于电力系统暂态稳定评估和临界切除时间（CCT）估计，通过精确事件链式结构和可微稳定裕度提升精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCT估计依赖大量重复仿真，计算昂贵且难以准确刻画故障清除动态，现有神经代理模型在事件界面处精度不足。

Method: ES-PINN按预故障、故障中、清除后三个阶段结构化表示，强制事件界面精确状态链接；用平滑轨迹诱导稳定裕度定义CCT边界的可微近似，支持边界提取、灵敏度分析和直接预测，并给出局部残差到轨迹再到CCT的误差界。

Result: 在IEEE 9、14、30节点系统上，ES-PINN在留出轨迹和稳定边界精度上持续优于匹配的神经代理基线，全网络DAE验证、多故障实验和运行时分析均证实有效性和计算效率。

Conclusion: ES-PINN通过事件结构和可微稳定裕度，为暂态稳定评估提供高精度、高计算效率的框架，并具备理论误差保证。

Abstract: Transient-stability assessment determines whether a power system can recover after a disturbance and is therefore essential to preventing generator trips and cascading outages. A key metric is the critical clearing time (CCT), which specifies the maximum time available to clear a fault before synchronism is lost. Reliable CCT estimation is challenging because complicated fault-clearing dynamics require repeated simulations over many fault severities and clearing times. We propose an event-structured physics-informed neural network (ES-PINN) that aligns its representation with the pre-fault, fault-on, and post-clearing swing dynamics and enforces exact state chaining across event interfaces. A smooth trajectory-induced stability margin defines a differentiable approximation of the CCT boundary, enabling accurate boundary extraction, local sensitivity analysis, and optional direct CCT prediction through a distilled readout. We further prove a local residual-to-trajectory-to-CCT error estimate, in which exact event chaining eliminates separate state-interface defect terms. Experiments on IEEE 9-, 14-, and 30-bus systems show that ES-PINN consistently improves held-out trajectory and stability-boundary accuracy over matched neural-surrogate baselines across mechanical and electrical contingencies with multiple clearing configurations. Additional full-network DAE validation, multi-fault experiments, and runtime analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness and computational efficiency of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [242] [NMINE: Normalized Mutual Information Neural Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27710)
*Petra Eerikinharju,Marko Tuononen,Ville Hautamäki*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了一种全神经网络的连续变量归一化互信息估计器，结合MINE和MI-NEE方法，在1-8维高斯数据上优于KSG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归一化互信息估计器对维度和数值稳定性敏感，且基于k近邻的方法在连续多维场景下精度有限，需要更稳健的神经网络替代方案。

Method: 利用Donsker-Varadhan表示估计互信息，通过学习各边际分布与均匀参考分布间的散度来恢复边际熵，进而计算归一化互信息。

Result: 在1到8维高斯数据实验中，所提估计器相比KSG基线提升了准确性。

Conclusion: 神经网络估计是连续多维归一化依赖度量的有前景方向。

Abstract: Mutual information is a general measure of statistical dependence that captures both linear and nonlinear relationships between random variables. For continuous and multidimensional variables For continuous multidimensional variables, mutual information must be estimated from samples. Because mutual information is unbounded, its values are not directly comparable across datasets, dimensions, or applications. Normalized mutual information addresses this limitation by converting mutual information into a normalized dependency score. Recent work has demonstrated the practical value of normalized mutual information in applications such as molecular dynamics {arXiv:2405.04980} and interpretable machine learning {arXiv:2409.16768}, but existing estimators remain sensitive to dimensionality and numerical stability {arXiv:2410.07642}.
  In this paper, we propose a fully neural normalized mutual information estimator for continuous variables. The proposed approach combines a MINE-based neural mutual information estimator {arXiv:1801.04062} with MI-NEE-inspired neural marginal entropy estimators {arXiv:1905.12957}. Mutual information is estimated using the Donsker--Varadhan representation, while marginal entropies are estimated by learning the divergence between each marginal distribution and a uniform reference distribution, from which entropy is recovered. The resulting estimator provides a neural alternative to k-nearest-neighbor-based normalized mutual information estimation {arXiv:2405.04980}.
  Experiments on Gaussian data from one to eight dimensions show that the proposed estimator improves accuracy over a KSG-based normalized mutual information baseline. These results indicate that neural estimation is a promising direction for normalized dependency measurement in continuous multidimensional settings.

</details>


### [243] [RIPPLE: Generating Multi-Channel Phase, Not Recovering It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27775)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Yeajin Lee,Dayeon Shin,Donghun Lee*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出RIPPLE框架，将Griffin-Lim从最终相位估计器重新定义为相位先验，结合整流流和显式通道间相位损失来生成多通道相位，在声学环境和地震数据上优于基于恢复的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型仅合成幅度谱，相位恢复按通道独立处理，导致多通道波形（如空间音频、三分量地震图）的通道间相位关系被破坏，而常用幅度指标无法反映该退化，造成物理信息丢失但评分良好的假象。

Method: 将Griffin-Lim视为相位先验（初始化自源相位），保留通道间结构；用整流流（rectified flow）优化相位，并在训练中引入显式通道间相位一致性损失，实现相位生成而非恢复。

Result: 在首阶环境转移和地震跨站翻译两类任务中，RIPPLE在相干性指标上超过基于恢复的管线；地震案例中，每通道恢复的S波偏振误差接近随机期望57.3°，而学习相位降至33.8°。

Conclusion: 相位应被生成而非恢复，RIPPLE证明显式建模通道间相位相干性可显著提升下游物理分析所需的输出质量，且方法跨领域有效。

Abstract: Generative models synthesize magnitude spectra with high fidelity, while phase is delegated to a recovery module---Griffin--Lim, a vocoder, or a latent decoder---applied independently to each channel. For multi-channel waveforms this delegation is costly: the physical content of spatial audio and three-component seismograms lives in the phase relationships between channels, precisely what channel-independent recovery cannot produce. The cost is also invisible, since the magnitude-based metrics common to both fields barely move when inter-channel phase coherence collapses---so a pipeline can discard the physical information in its output while still scoring well. We argue that phase should be generated, not recovered, and present RIPPLE (Rectified Inter-channel Phase with Prior-based LEarning), which reinterprets Griffin--Lim as a phase **prior** rather than a final estimator: initialized from the source phase, this prior carries the inter-channel structure to be preserved, and a rectified flow refines it toward the target under an explicit inter-channel phase loss. Tested on first-order ambisonics environment transfer and seismic cross-station translation---two physically unrelated domains---RIPPLE outperforms recovery-based pipelines on the coherence metrics that downstream analyses consume. The seismic case is decisive: across architecturally distinct generators, per-channel recovery leaves S-wave polarization error near the $57.3^\circ$ random expectation, whereas learned phase reduces it to $33.8^\circ$.

</details>


### [244] [DataClawEval: A Benchmark for Data Engineering Agents in Real Industrial Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28033)
*Debin Meng,Jiaming Yang,Zefang Zong,Tengyue Xu,Haining Xie,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DataClawEval，首个针对真实数据工程场景中自主智能体端到端任务完成能力的综合基准，含100个任务、5种执行引擎，用确定性规则脚本评估，发现最强模型仅达74.9分且无模型全能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦简化的Text-to-SQL或数据分析，忽略端到端数据工程这一关键复杂领域，需填补空白以评估自主智能体在真实生产环境中的实际能力。

Method: 基于专业企业数据工程师编写的生产级代码构建100个端到端任务，覆盖PySpark、MySQL、HiveSQL、PrestoSQL/Trino、FlinkSQL五种引擎；每个任务在独立沙箱中执行，用确定性规则脚本而非LLM-as-a-judge打分。

Result: 评估16个前沿智能体，最强仅74.9总分，无单一模型在所有引擎上占优，各模型在不同引擎上表现突出，显示严格领域专长而非通用能力。

Conclusion: 自主数据工程仍是艰巨未解挑战，当前智能体能力存在显著局限，该基准为后续研究提供基础设施和评估标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed to automate complex workflows, promising to revolutionize data management and processing. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on simplified Text-to-SQL translation or data analysis, leaving the critical and complex domain of end-to-end data engineering largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce DataClawEval, the first comprehensive benchmark designed specifically to evaluate the end-to-end task completion capabilities of autonomous agents in real-world data engineering scenarios. Built upon production-grade code authored by professional enterprise data engineers, it comprises 100 rigorous, end-to-end tasks spanning five execution engines: PySpark, MySQL, HiveSQL, PrestoSQL/Trino, and FlinkSQL. Rather than non-deterministic LLM-as-a-judge scoring, each task is executed within a case-specific, isolated sandbox and graded by deterministic, rule-based scripts. Evaluating 16 frontier agents exposes critical limitations: The strongest model attains only 74.9 overall, and no single model dominates, as each excels on a different engine, revealing strict domain specialization rather than omnipotent proficiency. Thus, autonomous data engineering remains a formidable, unresolved challenge. We release our dataset, containerized environments, and deterministic evaluation scripts at https://github.com/Dicemy/DataClawEval/tree/master

</details>


### [245] [Distilling Answer Set Programming Theories from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28086)
*Nelson Higuera Ruiz,Markus Hofmarcher,Claudiu Leoveanu-Condrei*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据集无关的神经符号协议，在固定智能体框架和求解器闭环下，仅用单次提示和空文件起始，模型需在1小时内从零蒸馏出完整正确的ASP理论。在CLEVR、GQA、CLEVRER三个VQA基准上测试9种模型，发现前沿模型（除GPT-5外）能接近或达到很高准确率，而GPT-5在部分任务上表现不稳，参考理论迁移会干扰其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零编写ASP理论困难且耗时，现有方法缺乏对模型自动蒸馏完整理论能力的系统研究，且不同模型规模对该任务的影响未知，需要一种通用、可复现的协议来评估。

Method: 采用固定智能体框架，以空文件为起点，模型通过单次提示在1小时内迭代生成并验证ASP理论（求解器在环）。在三个公开VQA基准上测试9个不同规模和来源的模型，并额外进行参考理论迁移实验。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.7、DeepSeek V4 Pro在CLEVR达100%，GQA达92.8%-98.8%，CLEVRER达92.7%-95.3%；GPT-5在CLEVR达98.7%但GQA骤降至41.8%，CLEVRER为86.7%。加入手写参考理论后，其余前沿模型变化在±3.4个百分点内，但GPT-5下降3-19个百分点。

Conclusion: 前沿模型能够自动蒸馏出高准确的ASP理论，但模型间差异显著，GPT-5对参考理论迁移敏感，表明该任务对模型类型和训练分布有明显依赖性；开放数据和代码可用于复现和扩展。

Abstract: Writing Answer Set Programming (ASP) theories from scratch is a difficult and time-consuming task. We take a neurosymbolic approach to study whether a model can distill complete and correct theories, given a fixed agent harness with the solver in the loop. The protocol is dataset-agnostic: with a single prompt and an empty file as the starting point the model is given a 1-hour time limit to derive a complete theory. We chose VQA as the application domain, three benchmarks (CLEVR, GQA, CLEVRER), as these are publicly available and non-trivial. In order to study the model scale required for solving this task we nine different models: four frontier (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.7, GPT-5, DeepSeek V4 Pro), two mid-tier (DeepSeek V4 Flash, gpt-oss-120b), and three open-weights (qwen3.6-27b, gpt-oss-20b, qwen3.5-9b). Three of four frontier models reach 100% on CLEVR and 92.8%-98.8% on GQA; on CLEVRER, Sonnet, Opus, DeepSeek V4 Pro score 92.7%-95.3%. GPT-5 reaches 98.7% on CLEVR but drops to 41.8% on GQA and to 86.7% on CLEVRER. Adding handwritten reference theories from other datasets moves the other three frontier models by at most +/-3.4 pp but reduces GPT-5's accuracy by 3-19 pp. We release the code, prompts, and theories distilled.

</details>


### [246] [It's All Just Vectorization: einx, a Universal Notation for Tensor Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27987)
*Florian Fervers,Sebastian Bullinger,Christoph Bodensteiner,Michael Arens*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出einx，一种基于向量化（vectorization）概念的通用张量操作表示法，通过声明式点式表达式（pointful expressions）简化张量编程，减少API复杂度并统一规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量框架（如NumPy）的表示法难以阅读和编写，易产生形状错误；einsum和einops虽受欢迎但仅限少数操作，缺乏通用性。需一种更普适的张量编程范式。

Method: 将向量化视为变换张量操作的函数，既能将低阶操作提升为高阶操作，也可将高阶操作分解为低阶操作及其向量化。基于此引入einx，使用与循环记号类比的定义清晰的声明式点式表达式，将大型API约简为少量基本操作，并嵌入Python与现有框架集成。

Result: einx提供了统一、一致的规则，降低API复杂度，显著提升代码的可读性和可写性。已实现Python嵌入版本，与主流张量框架无缝集成。

Conclusion: einx为张量编程提供了更通用、更简洁的表示法，有望替代现有复杂且易错的 notation，成为更优的通用范式。

Abstract: Tensor operations represent a cornerstone of modern scientific computing. However, the Numpy-like notation adopted by predominant tensor frameworks is often difficult to read and write and prone to so-called shape errors, i.a., due to following inconsistent rules across a large, complex collection of operations. Alternatives like einsum and einops have gained popularity, but are inherently restricted to few operations and lack the generality required for a universal model of tensor programming.
  To derive a better paradigm, we revisit vectorization as a function for transforming tensor operations, and use it to both lift lower-order operations to higher-order operations, and conceptually decompose higher-order operations to lower-order operations and their vectorization.
  Building on the universal nature of vectorization, we introduce einx, a universal notation for tensor operations. It uses declarative, pointful expressions that are defined by analogy with loop notation and represent the vectorization of tensor operations. The notation reduces the large APIs of existing frameworks to a small set of elementary operations, applies consistent rules across all operations, and enables a clean, readable and writable representation in code. We provide an implementation of einx that is embedded in Python and integrates seamlessly with existing tensor frameworks: https://github.com/fferflo/einx

</details>


### [247] [BlueprintRepair: Typed Local Edits for Failed Lean Proof Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28110)
*Ruslan Khrulev*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueprintRepair，一种基于蓝图依赖图的定理证明修复接口，通过十种模式检查的局部操作让模型修改证明结构；并构建Benchmark BlueprintTrace，含142个受控失败及完整修复轨迹。实验表明，在匹配条件下，类型化编辑接口在解决局部失败上与其他接口相当，但成本最低，且在预算内覆盖率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的Lean证明系统将证明组织为蓝图（形式语句依赖图），但缺乏让模型以受控方式修改该图以修复失败证明的接口。直接源码补丁或模块重写成本高且可能改变目标定理，因此需要一种既能精确编辑又保证目标不变的修复方式。

Method: 设计BlueprintRepair，提供十种经模式检查的局部操作，操作需指定目标节点，Lean验证每次修改，并要求修复声明使用的所有蓝图引理。构建BlueprintTrace基准，含142个失败案例及完整接受/拒绝轨迹。比较三种接口：类型化编辑、精确源码补丁、完整模块重写，在匹配源、反馈、模型、预算下进行单episode实验。

Result: 使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash时，三种接口解决局部失败数量几乎相同，但类型化修复每个解决状态成本最低（补丁贵1.30倍，重写贵2.06倍），且在10,000 token预算内达到几乎全部最终覆盖率，自由形式接口大幅落后。Qwen3.6-Flash解决更少状态但保持类型化最便宜，在证明编写状态上领先，重复局部失败模式。

Conclusion: BlueprintRepair的类型化局部编辑在修复局部失败上高效且经济，相比自由形式接口在预算内覆盖率更高，且跨模型表现一致，表明结构化蓝图操作是LLM证明修复的有效范式。

Abstract: LLM-based Lean proving systems increasingly organize a proof as a blueprint: a dependency graph of formal statements. We introduce BlueprintRepair, a repair interface that lets a model change this graph through ten schema-checked local operations. An operation names the node it edits, so the target theorem cannot be changed. Lean checks every applied change, and an accepted repair must declare every blueprint lemma its proof uses. We also construct BlueprintTrace, a benchmark of 142 controlled failures with complete accepted and rejected repair trajectories. We compare typed edits, exact source patches, and complete module rewrites under matched source, feedback, model, and budget, one episode per state and interface. With DeepSeek-V4-Flash, the three interfaces solve almost the same number of the benchmark's localized failures. Typed repair is the cheapest per solved state (patching is 1.30x as expensive, rewriting 2.06x), and within 10,000 completion tokens per task it reaches almost all of its final coverage, while both free-form interfaces are well behind. A second model, Qwen3.6-Flash, solves fewer states but keeps typed repair cheapest, puts it ahead on the proof-authoring states, and repeats the localized pattern.

</details>


### [248] [Learning features from Newton's algorithm: a way to accelerate nonlinear parametrized PDE solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28036)
*Rémy Vallot,Florian de Vuyst,Thibault Dairay,Mathilde Mougeot*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段牛顿初始猜测策略，通过构造解特征空间和修正方向特征空间，结合回归预测与GMRES残差最小化修正，为高保真牛顿法提供优质初始点，从而减少迭代次数和CPU时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿法的收敛速度高度依赖初始猜测的精度，现有基于代理模型的初始化方法仍难以在复杂非线性系统中提供足够好的初值，导致迭代次数多、计算成本高。

Method: 基于参数空间采样和预计算解数据库，利用离散牛顿轨迹构建两个互补降阶空间：收敛态构成的解特征空间和牛顿增量构成的修正搜索方向特征空间；对未知参数先用回归模型预测近似解，再通过GMRES求解一个小规模最小二乘问题获得残差最小化修正，最终作为高保真牛顿法的初始猜测。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法显著减少牛顿迭代次数并降低总CPU时间，相较于独立代理初始化获得可量化的加速，且在多类代表性PDE问题上观察到明显提速。

Conclusion: 该弱侵入式通用策略可广泛适用于大规模非线性问题，在已有残差场和脚本接口条件下能有效提升求解效率。

Abstract: It is well known that Newton's method converges faster when the initial guess is closer to a root of a system of nonlinear equations. In this paper, a two-stage Newton initial guess strategy is proposed by learning features from a parameter-space sampling and a database of precomputed solutions. The method uses discrete Newton trajectories to construct two complementary reduced spaces: a solution feature space, built from converged states, and a corrective search direction feature space, built from intermediate Newton increments. For an unseen parameter, a regression model is used to predict a surrogate solution approximation. Then, in a second step, a residual-minimizing correction is computed using a dedicated GMRES-based approach. The resulting state is then used as an initial guess for the high-fidelity Newton method, which completes convergence. The corrective step is computationally inexpensive since it only requires residual evaluations and the solution of a small least-squares problem. The methodology is weakly intrusive once the high-fidelity residual fields and a script-based programming interface are available. This strategy reduces the number of Newton iterations and decreases the overall CPU time. Numerical experiments on representative PDE problems show quantifiable speedups compared with standalone surrogate initialization. Significant speedups are observed. This generic approach can be applied to a broad class of large-scale nonlinear problems.

</details>


### [249] [AI and Authenticity in Islamic Research: A Critical Evaluation of Generative AI Reliability, Hallucination, and Source Fidelity in Quranic, Hadith, and Fiqh Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28237)
*Muhammad Sajjad Akbar*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究评估了六款主流生成式AI系统在伊斯兰知识领域（古兰经注释、圣训、教法、伦理等）的准确性、引用真实性、幻觉率、学派一致性及地域差异，发现AI可作为辅助工具，但不可替代权威宗教来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穆斯林日益依赖生成式AI获取宗教指导，但缺乏实证研究验证其提供的伊斯兰知识是否真实、可验证且值得信赖，尤其是在高信任度的宗教场景下。

Method: 采用混合方法，向澳大利亚和英国参与者提出50个涵盖多领域的开放式伊斯兰问题，收集六款AI的真实响应，分析领域准确性、引用验证、幻觉、教法学派一致性、不确定性处理、来源溯源及地域差异。

Result: AI系统在入门级伊斯兰学习中有辅助价值，但在教法裁决、学术研究等场景下存在明显局限，包括幻觉、不完整引用、学派一致性不足等问题。

Conclusion: 生成式AI不应被视为宗教裁决或伊斯兰研究的权威来源，必须对照经过验证的原始文献和合格学者意见进行核实；研究为开发者、教育者和穆斯林群体提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used by Muslims for religious guidance, Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith explanation, jurisprudential rulings, and Islamic education. Despite its growing adoption, there is limited empirical evidence on whether current AI systems provide authentic, verifiable, and trustworthy Islamic knowledge suitable for high-trust religious contexts. This study evaluates six leading generative AI systems using fifty realistic open-ended Islamic questions covering Qur'anic interpretation, Hadith, Fiqh, ethics, pastoral advice, and Madhhab-sensitive topics. Responses were collected under real-world conditions from participants in Australia and the United Kingdom and analysed using a mixed-method framework examining domain accuracy, citation verification, hallucinations, jurisprudential consistency, uncertainty handling, source provenance, and geographical variation.
  The study addresses four research questions: (1) How accurate and authentic are AI-generated responses across major Islamic knowledge domains? (2) To what extent do AI systems produce hallucinations, incomplete citations, or unverifiable religious references? (3) How consistently do models handle jurisprudential disagreement, Madhhab diversity, and uncertainty? (4) Are current AI systems sufficiently reliable for religious guidance, Islamic education, and scholarly research?
  Overall, current generative AI systems are valuable as assistive tools for introductory Islamic learning but should not be treated as authoritative sources for religious rulings or Islamic research without verification against authenticated primary sources and qualified scholarly expertise. This study provides one of the first comprehensive empirical evaluations of AI reliability within Islamic knowledge, offering practical guidance for researchers, educators, AI developers, and the wider Muslim community.

</details>


### [250] [LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search for Constraint Model Reformulation to Improve Solver Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28268)
*Kostis Michailidis,Dimos Tsouros,Nguyen Dang,Tias Guns*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用大语言模型（LLM）结合进化框架自动化重构组合优化问题的约束模型，以提升求解性能；提出基于最大边际相关的Profile-Diverse Retention（PDR）策略保留行为多样化的历史尝试，实验表明该策略优于仅保留最近或最快尝试的方法，且基于验证集的选择能稳定提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合问题在工业中广泛存在，通常建模为约束模型并交由后端求解器求解。即使LLM能生成正确模型，不同建模选择对求解效率影响巨大，而手动调优耗时费力。现有自动启发式设计（AHD）方法虽可借助LLM进行重构，但上下文保留策略缺乏显式多样性，导致搜索易陷入局部最优。

Method: 采用进化搜索框架，LLM作为变异算子生成候选重构模型，通过基准测试验证并反馈性能。设计了多种上下文保留策略，并新提出PDR：基于实例级运行时间向量，利用最大边际相关性（MMR）选择行为多样且相关的历史尝试。在8个CSPLib问题上系统评估，最终模型通过验证集选择确定。

Result: 实验显示：（i）迭代重构可显著提升留出集（held-out）求解速度；（ii）保留行为多样性上下文的策略优于仅保留最近或最快尝试的策略；（iii）基于验证集的模型选择策略对所有方法的留出集加速均有正向提升。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的进化式模型重构能有效优化约束模型性能，且上下文多样性至关重要；PDR策略与验证集选择结合可进一步提升泛化能力，为自动化模型调优提供了实用而稳健的路线。

Abstract: Combinatorial problems appear in numerous industrial applications. A common approach is to formulate these problems as declarative constraint models that can subsequently be compiled to and solved by a range of back-end solvers. Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can produce correct models from natural language, but even a correct model can be expensive to solve because performance remains sensitive to modelling choices. In this work, we investigate whether LLMs can automate performance-oriented model reformulation. Inspired by Automatic Heuristic Design (AHD), we use an evolutionary framework in which an LLM proposes candidate reformulations that are verified and benchmarked against the user-defined baseline model. We compare AHD-adapted search strategies that control which prior attempts, instructions, and measured feedback enter each prompt. Existing retention strategies prioritize recency or performance, but do not explicitly diversify the context. To cover this gap, we introduce Profile-Diverse Retention (PDR), which applies Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) to instance-level runtime vectors to retain behaviourally diverse attempts. We systematically evaluate the strategies on eight CSPLib problems using validation-based final model selection. The results show that: (i) iterative reformulation can produce substantial held-out speedups; (ii) strategies that keep the retained context diverse outperform those that retain only recent or the fastest attempts; and (iii) validation-based selection improves the held-out speedup of every strategy.

</details>


### [251] [Search Strategies for Optimal Classification and Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28170)
*Jacobus G. M. van der Linden,Mim van den Bos,Emir Demirović*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出统一框架比较ODT搜索策略，发现最优策略在分类和回归任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ODT研究缺乏对搜索策略贡献的系统分析，难以指导算法设计。

Method: 构建通用算法框架，实例化18种搜索策略并实证比较其效果。

Result: 最佳策略在分类上提升任意时刻性能，回归上提速超一个数量级。

Conclusion: 统一框架有助于理解策略差异，所提策略大幅提升ODT可扩展性。

Abstract: Optimal decision trees (ODTs) are compact, interpretable machine learning models that globally optimize a given objective, but their scalability remains challenging. While recent work has proposed a variety of search strategies to improve scalability, the precise contribution of each strategy remains unclear. To address this gap, we introduce a general algorithmic framework for ODTs that instantiates previously used search strategies and enables the definition of new ones. This provides a common lens through which to understand and compare different strategies, which we use to empirically investigate the effect of 18 search strategies. Compared to the state of the art, the best strategy in our evaluation achieves significantly better anytime performance for classification, and improves runtime by more than an order of magnitude for regression.

</details>


### [252] [Persistent Gaussian Perturbations Prevent Oversmoothing in Recurrent Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28185)
*Mostafa Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文证明在循环图神经网络中注入独立高斯噪声可使隐藏表示形成几何遍历马尔可夫链，其平稳分布的期望Dirichlet能量有正下界，从而严格防止渐近过平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度GNN因消息传递导致节点表示趋同（过平滑），限制网络深度。现有缓解方法多为确定性机制（残差、归一化、重连），本文探索随机扰动作为根本不同的替代方案。

Method: 将带噪声注入的循环GNN建模为随机动力系统，在全局收缩假设下证明马尔可夫链几何遍历性，并推导平稳Dirichlet能量的显式下界（与噪声方差和图谱间隙成比例）。

Result: 理论证明平稳表示不会坍缩到常数流形；数值实验在线性和非线性循环GNN上验证了平稳分布存在及能量对噪声强度的依赖预测。

Conclusion: 持续随机扰动是对抗过平滑的有效新机制，为设计深度GNN提供了概率论视角的严格保证和理论指导。

Abstract: Oversmoothing is a fundamental limitation of deep graph neural networks (GNNs), where repeated message passing causes node representations to become increasingly similar, eventually collapsing toward a low-dimensional subspace. This phenomenon limits the effective depth of message-passing architectures and motivates the search for mechanisms that preserve representation diversity. In this paper, we study a recurrent graph neural network in which independent Gaussian noise is injected after every propagation step and analyze the resulting architecture as a stochastic dynamical system. Under a standard global contraction assumption on the deterministic update, we prove that the hidden representations form a geometrically ergodic Markov chain admitting a unique invariant probability measure. Our main theoretical result establishes an explicit positive lower bound on the expected stationary Dirichlet energy, proportional to both the noise variance and the spectral gap of the underlying graph. Consequently, the stationary representations cannot collapse onto the constant manifold, providing a rigorous guarantee that asymptotic oversmoothing is prevented in the sense of non-vanishing Dirichlet energy. Our analysis reveals persistent stochastic perturbations as a fundamentally different mechanism for combating oversmoothing, complementing existing deterministic approaches based on residual connections, normalization, and graph rewiring. Finally, numerical experiments on both linear and nonlinear recurrent graph neural networks closely match the theoretical predictions, illustrating the emergence of a stationary distribution and the predicted dependence of the limiting Dirichlet energy on the noise intensity.

</details>


### [253] [TopoFormer: Topology Meets Attention for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28259)
*Md Joshem Uddin,Astrit Tola,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Topoformer，通过Topo-Scan模块将图结构编码为拓扑token序列，供Transformer处理，实现轻量、可并行且稳定的图表示学习，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持久同调计算成本高、难以并行且与深度学习框架集成困难，而现有GNN在图拓扑结构捕捉上存在局限，需要一种既能引入拓扑归纳偏置又高效可扩展的图表示学习方法。

Method: 设计Topo-Scan模块，基于节点或边过滤将图分解为有序的短拓扑token序列，捕获从局部模体到全局组织的多尺度结构模式；序列输入Transformer生成图级嵌入，并提供理论稳定性保证。

Result: 在图分类和分子性质预测基准上匹配或超过强GNN与拓扑方法基线，同时计算可预测且高效，验证了并行化拓扑编码的有效性。

Conclusion: Topoformer开辟了将拓扑结构融入注意力框架的并行化统一路径，为图表示学习提供了新思路，兼具性能与效率优势。

Abstract: We introduce Topoformer, a lightweight and scalable framework for graph representation learning that encodes topological structure into attention-friendly sequences. At the core of our method is Topo-Scan, a novel module that decomposes a graph into a short, ordered sequence of topological tokens by slicing over node or edge filtrations. These sequences capture multi-scale structural patterns, from local motifs to global organization, and are processed by a Transformer to produce expressive graph-level embeddings. Unlike traditional persistent homology pipelines, Topo-Scan is parallelizable, avoids costly diagram computations, and integrates seamlessly with standard deep learning architectures. We provide theoretical guarantees on the stability of our topological encodings and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across graph classification and molecular property prediction benchmarks. Our results show that Topoformer matches or exceeds strong GNN and topology-based baselines while offering predictable and efficient compute. This work opens a new path for parallelizable and unifying approaches to graph representation learning that integrate topological inductive biases into attention frameworks.

</details>


### [254] [Measuring Distortion in the Empty Regions of Dimensionality Reduction Scatterplots with the Gap Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28324)
*Jaume Ros,Alessio Arleo,Fernando Paulovich*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种新的二维投影质量度量指标Gap Index (GI)，聚焦投影空白区域的视觉形变，通过三角剖分比较高低维空间的空三角形变形来量化失真，能捕捉传统指标忽略的细微结构变化，兼具计算效率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影质量指标（如距离/邻域保持）仅关注点间直接关系，忽略布局中空白区域的形变，而空白区域往往构成视觉上重要的特征（如簇间空隙），导致对高视觉影响的结构变化不敏感。

Method: 将投影空间分解为空白三角形（空三角剖分），将每个三角形与其高维对应体进行比较，计算局部形变；形变可聚合为标量值，或叠加在投影上显示区域性失真分布。

Result: 与流行指标相比，GI对具有高视觉冲击的小型结构变形更敏感，且计算快速、结果易于解释。

Conclusion: GI提供了一种补充现有度量的新视角，能更全面评估投影质量，尤其适用于强调空白区域结构特征的视觉分析场景。

Abstract: Quality metrics play a crucial role in the proper use of dimensionality reduction projections for visual analysis of high-dimensional data. They quantify the degree of distortion of a projection compared to the high-dimensional data and provide a reliable indication of how confident users can be in the structures they see in the resulting layouts. However, most popular metrics focus on capturing direct relationships between points (e.g., distances or neighborhoods) while neglecting distortions in empty areas of the layout, even though these often compose visually relevant features of a 2D layout. In this paper, we introduce the Gap Index (GI), a quality metric for 2D projections that captures visual distortion by measuring spatial distortion in empty areas of a projection. It does so by decomposing the space into empty triangles, which are then compared to their high-dimensional counterparts to compute the deformation. This per-triangle deformation can be aggregated into a single scalar value or overlaid on a projection to visualize regional distortion patterns. Results show that, contrary to popular quality metrics, the GI is sensitive to small structural deformations that have high visual impact. It is also fast to compute and interpretable.

</details>


### [255] [When Specifications Conflict: A Symmetry-Based Framework for Measuring LLM Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28384)
*Tairan Wang,Liang Zhou,Zikang Zhan,Pingchuan Yan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出可控实验框架，系统测量LLM在冲突规范间的偏好，发现一致偏好顺序（形式语言≈自然化形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例），并验证框架在多个领域的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需整合多源可能冲突的信息，但缺乏可控、可归因的方法分析模型如何解决规范冲突，现有研究难以直接观察和比较模型在不同表示类型下的选择偏好。

Method: 构建显式冲突的规范对，采用对称性设计消除混淆因素，在可执行数学基准（550个冲突实例、11个函数族）上比较纯自然语言、形式语言、自然化形式语言和输入-输出示例四种表示类型，并扩展到布尔代数、代码生成和临床领域的异构冲突。

Result: 模型表现出系统偏好而非随机行为，排序为：形式≈自然化形式>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例；示例效应依赖模型能力与函数族；框架在多领域均适用。

Conclusion: 该框架提供统一方法测量LLM冲突解决偏好，支持跨表示类型和任务的系统比较，为理解模型信息整合机制提供可扩展工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly required to integrate multiple sources of information that may be inconsistent or conflicting. However, there is still a lack of controllable and attributable methods for analyzing how models resolve conflicts between competing specifications. We propose a controlled experimental framework for studying model preferences under conflicting specifications. By constructing specifications with explicit conflicts, the framework enables model choices between competing specifications to be directly observed and analyzed. A symmetry-based design further reduces confounding factors, allowing preferences across representation types to be compared systematically.
  We evaluate the framework on an executable mathematical benchmark with 550 conflict instances spanning 11 function families, comparing four representation types: pure natural language, formal language, naturalized formal language, and input--output examples. Results show systematic preference patterns rather than random behavior, with a consistent ordering: $ \text{Formal} \approx \text{Naturalized Formal} > \text{Pure Natural Language} > \text{Input--Output Examples} $. Example effects further depend on model capability and function family.
  We extend the framework to heterogeneous specification conflicts in Boolean algebra, code generation, and the clinical domain, demonstrating its applicability across diverse tasks and specification forms. The framework provides a unified approach for measuring how LLMs resolve conflicts between competing sources of information.

</details>


### [256] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模动作空间下上下文赌博机的策略学习，分别提出用于在线学习的结构化贝叶斯方法（meTS、dTS）和用于离线学习的结构化直接方法、可优化目标及悲观正则化方法，兼顾理论保证与实际效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型动作空间中，在线和离线策略学习均面临探索效率低、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重方差高、外推偏差大及优化困难等挑战，现有方法难以有效共享动作间信息或平衡偏差与方差。

Method: 在线学习部分提出混合效应汤普森采样（meTS）和基于扩散先验的dTS，通过结构化先验建模动作依赖；离线学习部分提出基于隐变量的结构化直接方法（sDM），设计凹的可优化策略加权对数似然目标，并引入基于指数平滑与PAC-Bayes界的可微悲观方法。

Result: 所提方法在理论上给出依赖于有效动作数的遗憾界，识别出大规模动作空间中优化误差可能主导估计误差，并通过悲观正则化有效控制重要性采样估计的偏差-方差权衡。

Conclusion: 结构化建模与悲观正则化是应对大规模动作空间策略学习核心挑战的有效途径，为在线与离线场景提供了兼具理论保证和实用性的算法框架。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [257] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出KSSE模型，用稀疏图谱嵌入替代密集CNN分类器，在ImageNet-1000上以21.24M参数达到88.93% Top-1精度，超越Swin-L并匹配ViT-H，模型规模缩减10-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集CNN分类器参数冗余且计算成本高，而图神经网络在特征嵌入上具备结构化优势。受统计物理启发，利用随机键伊辛模型的西森温度下的谱性质，构建物理驱动的轻量级分类框架。

Method: 将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构造Kohn-Sham哈密顿量的正则化拉普拉斯算子；利用FFT在循环块上求解D个独立通道谱问题；通过star-domain surgery优化图拓扑，保证局部凸性并限制挫折；结合多重分形分析验证景观相变并指导Rayleigh细化。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000冻结EfficientNet-B4特征上取得88.93% Top-1精度，参数仅21.24M，性能超过Swin-L（197M）并匹配ViT-H/14（632M），模型规模分别缩减10倍和30倍。

Conclusion: 物理启发的谱嵌入方法可高效替代密集分类器，验证了在西森温度下图谱嵌入的有效性，为构建极低参数、高精度的分类模型提供新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [258] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: EndoCLIP是一种基于结肠镜影像与文本报告训练的视觉-语言基础模型，通过从常规报告中恢复病灶级图文对，在多项临床任务中优于通用和医学专用模型，且线性探针在良恶性分类上接近专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管常规结肠镜报告中包含丰富的专家描述（如病灶外观、大小、位置），但这些描述是针对整个手术过程而非单帧图像，导致临床发现与对应图像之间缺乏强关联，限制了视觉-语言模型在结肠镜中的应用。

Method: 从280,476份常规结肠镜报告中渐进恢复出125,756个病灶级图像-文本对，并以此训练EndoCLIP模型，使其能够将临床目标以语言形式指定，而非为每个任务单独标注。

Result: 在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成及六个多中心临床分类任务中，EndoCLIP在零样本和线性探针设置下均优于通用及生物医学视觉-语言编码器；在良恶性分类任务中，其线性探针性能接近12名内镜医师的盲审结果。

Conclusion: 恢复发现与帧图像的对应关系可将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标能通过语言灵活定义，从而减少对逐任务人工标注的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [259] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出五阶段审计流水线KAISEN，通过合成基准压力测试发现：显著性需按最小可检测效应标准化；阈值优化稳定降低EOD而Platt缩放无效；机制诊断在代理误设下静默失效；漂移监控阈值在不同队列间不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型的群体公平性审计组件缺乏压力测试，各环节可信度及适用条件不明。

Method: 构建包含16种疾病、15个社会决定因素轴及3个预设交集的合成基准，对KAISEN五个阶段（分层、差异测量、机制诊断、事后缓解、漂移监控）进行到失败点的系统评估。

Result: 显著性-差距相关性经标准化从rho=0.56升至0.78；阈值优化在48/48次运行中降低EOD（均值差-0.285），Platt缩放仅19/48次改善；机制诊断144/144正确但代理误设下0/48恢复且无失败信号；漂移阈值跨队列失效（卡方p=0.002）。

Conclusion: 审计组件需独立验证，报告应强调方差而非均值，且所有结果仅限合成数据，不代表临床有效性。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>
